					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־עַל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ ט֔וֹב יִכָּתֵ֖ב לְאַבְּדָ֑ם וַעֲשֶׂ֨רֶת אֲלָפִ֜ים כִּכַּר־כֶּ֗סֶף אֶשְׁקוֹל֙ עַל־יְדֵי֙ עֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה לְהָבִ֖יא אֶל־גִּנְזֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἰ δοκεῖ τῷ βασιλεῖ, δογματισάτω ἀπολέσαι αὐτούς, κἀγὼ διαγράψω εἰς τὸ γαζοφυλάκιον τοῦ βασιλέως ἀργυρίου τάλαντα μύρια.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If it please the king, let it be written that they be destroyed: and I will pay ten thousand talents of silver into the hands of those that have the charge of the king's business, to bring it into the king's treasuries.
					\item[NIV] If it pleases the king, let a decree be issued to destroy them, and I will give ten thousand talents of silver to the king’s administrators for the royal treasury.”
					\item[NRSV] If it pleases the king, let a decree be issued for their destruction, and I will pay ten thousand talents of silver into the hands of those who have charge of the king’s business, so that they may put it into the king’s treasuries.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:10} % *ESTHER-3-10
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-10}

			And the king took his ring from his hand, and gave it unto Haman the son of Hammedatha the Agagite, the Jews’ enemy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֧סַר הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ אֶת־טַבַּעְתּ֖וֹ מֵעַ֣ל יָד֑וֹ וַֽיִּתְּנָ֗הּ לְהָמָ֧ן בֶּֽן־הַמְּדָ֛תָא הָאֲגָגִ֖י צֹרֵ֥ר הַיְּהוּדִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ περιελόμενος ὁ βασιλεὺς τὸν δακτύλιον ἔδωκεν εἰς χεῖρα τῷ Ἁμάν, σφραγίσαι κατὰ τῶν γεγραμμένων κατὰ τῶν Ἰουδαίων.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king took his ring from his hand, and gave it unto Haman the son of Hammedatha the Agagite, the Jews' enemy.
					\item[NIV] So the king took his signet ring from his finger and gave it to Haman son of Hammedatha, the Agagite, the enemy of the Jews.
					\item[NRSV] So the king took his signet ring from his hand and gave it to Haman son of Hammedatha the Agagite, the enemy of the Jews.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:11} % *ESTHER-3-11
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-11}

			And the king said unto Haman, The silver is given to thee, the people also, to do with them as it seemeth good to thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ לְהָמָ֔ן הַכֶּ֖סֶף נָת֣וּן לָ֑ךְ וְהָעָ֕ם לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת בּ֖וֹ כַּטּ֥וֹב בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεὺς τῷ Ἁμάν Τὸ μὲν ἀργύριον ἔχε, τῷ δὲ ἔθνει χρῶ ὡς βούλει.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said unto Haman, The silver is given to thee, the people also, to do with them as it seemeth good to thee.
					\item[NIV] “Keep the money,” the king said to Haman, “and do with the people as you please.”
					\item[NRSV] The king said to Haman, “The money is given to you, and the people as well, to do with them as it seems good to you.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:12} % *ESTHER-3-12
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-12}

			Then were the king’s scribes called on the thirteenth day of the first month, and there was written according to all that Haman had commanded unto the king’s lieutenants, and to the governors that were over every province, and to the rulers of every people of every province according to the writing thereof, and to every people after their language; in the name of king Ahasuerus was it written, and sealed with the king’s ring.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקָּרְאוּ֩ סֹפְרֵ֨י הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן בִּשְׁלוֹשָׁ֨ה עָשָׂ֣ר יוֹם֮ בּוֹ֒ וַיִּכָּתֵ֣ב כְּֽכָל־אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֣ה הָמָ֡ן אֶ֣ל אֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנֵֽי־הַ֠מֶּלֶךְ וְֽאֶל־הַפַּח֞וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר ׀ עַל־מְדִינָ֣ה וּמְדִינָ֗ה וְאֶל־שָׂ֤רֵי עַם֙ וָעָ֔ם מְדִינָ֤ה וּמְדִינָה֙ כִּכְתָבָ֔הּ וְעַ֥ם וָעָ֖ם כִּלְשׁוֹנ֑וֹ בְּשֵׁ֨ם הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ אֲחַשְׁוֵרֹשׁ֙ נִכְתָּ֔ב וְנֶחְתָּ֖ם בְּטַבַּ֥עַת הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐκλὴθησαν οἱ γραμματεῖς τοῦ βασιλέως μηνὶ πρώτῳ τῇ τρισκαιδεκάτῃ, καὶ ἔγραψαν ὡς ἐπέταξεν Ἁμὰν τοῖς στρατηγοῖς καὶ τοῖς ἄρχουσιν κατὰ πᾶσαν χώραν ἀπὸ Ἰνδικῆς ἕως τῆς Αἰθιοπίας, ταῖς ἑκατὸν εἴκοσι ἑπτὰ χώραις, τοῖς τε ἄρχουσι τῶν ἐθνῶν κατὰ τὴν αὐτῶν λέξιν, δι᾽ Ἀρταξέρξου τοῦ βασιλέως.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then were the king's scribes called in the first month, on the thirteenth day thereof; and there was written according to all that Haman commanded unto the king's satraps, and to the governors that were over every province, and to the princes of every people, to every province according to the writing thereof, and to every people after their language; in the name of king Ahasuerus was it written, and it was sealed with the king's ring.
					\item[NIV] Then on the thirteenth day of the first month the royal secretaries were summoned. They wrote out in the script of each province and in the language of each people all Haman’s orders to the king’s satraps, the governors of the various provinces and the nobles of the various peoples. These were written in the name of King Xerxes himself and sealed with his own ring.
					\item[NRSV] Then the king’s secretaries were summoned on the thirteenth day of the first month, and an edict, according to all that Haman commanded, was written to the king’s satraps and to the governors over all the provinces and to the officials of all the peoples, to every province in its own script and every people in its own language; it was written in the name of King Ahasuerus and sealed with the king’s ring.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:13} % *ESTHER-3-13
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-13}

			And the letters were sent by posts into all the king’s provinces, to destroy, to kill, and to cause to perish, all Jews, both young and old, little children and women, in one day, even upon the thirteenth day of the twelfth month, which is the month Adar, and to take the spoil of them for a prey.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְנִשְׁל֨וֹחַ סְפָרִ֜ים בְּיַ֣ד הָרָצִים֮ אֶל־כָּל־מְדִינ֣וֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ֒ לְהַשְׁמִ֡יד לַהֲרֹ֣ג וּלְאַבֵּ֣ד אֶת־כָּל־הַ֠יְּהוּדִים מִנַּ֨עַר וְעַד־זָקֵ֜ן טַ֤ף וְנָשִׁים֙ בְּי֣וֹם אֶחָ֔ד בִּשְׁלוֹשָׁ֥ה עָשָׂ֛ר לְחֹ֥דֶשׁ שְׁנֵים־עָשָׂ֖ר הוּא־חֹ֣דֶשׁ אֲדָ֑ר וּשְׁלָלָ֖ם לָבֽוֹז׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπεστάλη διὰ βιβλιαφόρων εἰς τὴν Ἀρταξέρξου βασιλείαν, ἀφανίσαι τὸ γένος τῶν Ἰουδαίων ἐν ἡμέρᾳ μιᾷ μηνὸς δωδεκάτου, ὅς ἐστιν Ἀδάρ, καὶ διαρπάσαι τὰ ὑπάρχοντα αὐτῶν. [1] Τῆς δὲ ἐπιστολῆς ἐστὶν τὸ ἀντίγραφον τόδε Βασιλεὺς μέγας Ἀρταξέρξης τοῖς ἀπὸ τῆς Ἰνδικῆς ἕως τῆς Αἰθιοπίας ἑκατὸν εἴκοσι ἑπτὰ χωρῶν ἄρχουσι καὶ τοπάρχαις ὑποτεταγμένοις τάδε γράφει. [2] πολλῶν ἐπάρξας ἐθνῶν καὶ πάσης ἐπικρατήσας οἰκουμένης, ἐβουλήθην, μὴ τῷ θράσει τῆς ἐξουσίας ἐπαιρόμενος ἐπιεικέστερον δὲ καὶ μετὰ ἠπιότητος ἀεὶ διεξάγων, τοὺς τῶν ὑποτεταγμένων ἀκυμάτους διὰ παντὸς καταστῆσαι βίους, τήν τε βασιλείαν ἥμερον καὶ πορευτὴν μέχρι περάτων παρεξόμενος ἀνανεώσασθαί τε τὴν ποθουμένην τοῖς πᾶσιν ἀνθρώποις εἰρήνην. [3] πυθομένου δέ μου τῶν συμβούλων πῶς ἂν ἀχθείη τοῦτο ἐπὶ πέρας, σωφροσύνῃ παρ᾽ ἡμῖν διενέγκας καὶ ἐν τῇ εὐνοίᾳ ἀπαραλλάκτως καὶ βεβαίᾳ πίστει ἀποδεδιγμένος καὶ δεύτερον τῶν βασιλειῶν γέρας ἀπενηνεγμένος Ἁμὰν [4] ἐπέδειξεν ἡμῖν, ἐν πάσαις ταῖς κατὰ τὴν οἰκουμένην φυλαῖς ἀναμεμίχθαι δυσμενῆ λαόν τινα, τοῖς νόμοις ἀντίθετον πρὸς πᾶν ἔθνος, τά τε τῶν βασιλέων παραπέμποντας διηνεκῶς προστάγματα, πρὸς τὸ μὴ κατατίθεσθαι τὴν ὑφ᾽ ἡμῶν κατευθυνομένην ἀμέμπτως συναρχίαν. [5] διειληφότες οὖν τόδε τὸ ἔθνος μονώτατον ἐν ἀντιπαραγωγῇ παντὶ διὰ παντὸς ἀνθρώπῳ κείμενον, διαγωγὴν νόμων ξενίζουσαν παραλλάσσον, καὶ δυσνοοῦν τοῖς ἡμετέροις πράγμασιν τὰ χείριστα συντελοῦν κακὰ καὶ πρὸς τὸ μὴ τὴν βασιλείαν εὐσταθίας τυγχάνειν· [6] προστετάχαμεν οὖν τοὺς σημαινομένους ὑμῖν ἐν τοῖς γεγραμμένοις ὑπὸ Ἁμὰν τοῦ τεταγμένου ἐπὶ τῶν πραγμάτων καὶ δευτέρου πατρὸς ἡμῶν, πάντας σὺν γυναιξὶ καὶ τέκνοις ἀπολέσαι ὁλορριζεὶ ταῖς τῶν ἐχθρῶν μαχαίραις ἄνευ παντὸς οἴκτου καὶ φειδοῦς τῇ τεσσαρεσκαιδεκαίτῃ τοῦ δωδεκάτου μηνὸς Ἀδὰρ τοῦ ἐνεστῶτος ἔτους, [7] ὅπως οἱ πάλαι καὶ νῦν δυσμενεῖς ἐν ἡμέρᾳ μιᾷ βιαίως εἰς τὸν ᾅδην κατελθόντες εἰς τὸν μετέπειτα χρόνον εὐσταθῆ καὶ ἀτάραχα παρέχωσιν ἡμῖν διὰ τέλους τὰ πράγματα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And letters were sent by posts into all the king's provinces, to destroy, to slay, and to cause to perish, all Jews, both young and old, little children and women, in one day, even upon the thirteenth day of the twelfth month, which is the month Adar, and to take the spoil of them for a prey.
					\item[NIV] Dispatches were sent by couriers to all the king’s provinces with the order to destroy, kill and annihilate all the Jews—young and old, women and children—on a single day, the thirteenth day of the twelfth month, the month of Adar, and to plunder their goods.
					\item[NRSV] Letters were sent by couriers to all the king’s provinces, giving orders to destroy, to kill, and to annihilate all Jews, young and old, women and children, in one day, the thirteenth day of the twelfth month, which is the month of Adar, and to plunder their goods.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:14} % *ESTHER-3-14
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-14}

			The copy of the writing for a commandment to be given in every province was published unto all people, that they should be ready against that day.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פַּתְשֶׁ֣גֶן הַכְּתָ֗ב לְהִנָּ֤תֵֽן דָּת֙ בְּכָל־מְדִינָ֣ה וּמְדִינָ֔ה גָּל֖וּי לְכָל־הָֽעַמִּ֑ים לִהְי֥וֹת עֲתִדִ֖ים לַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{τὰ δὲ ἀντίγραφα τῶν ἐπιστολῶν ἐξετίθετο κατὰ χώραν· καὶ προσετάγη πᾶσι τοῖς ἔθνεσιν ἑτοίμους εἶναι εἰς τὴν ἡμέραν ταύτην.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A copy of the writing, that the decree should be given out in every province, was published unto all the peoples, that they should be ready against that day.
					\item[NIV] A copy of the text of the edict was to be issued as law in every province and made known to the people of every nationality so they would be ready for that day.
					\item[NRSV] A copy of the document was to be issued as a decree in every province by proclamation, calling on all the peoples to be ready for that day.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 3:15} % *ESTHER-3-15
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-3-15}

			The posts went out, being hastened by the king’s commandment, and the decree was given in Shushan the palace. And the king and Haman sat down to drink; but the city Shushan was perplexed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָֽרָצִ֞ים יָצְא֤וּ דְחוּפִים֙ בִּדְבַ֣ר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְהַדָּ֥ת נִתְּנָ֖ה בְּשׁוּשַׁ֣ן הַבִּירָ֑ה וְהַמֶּ֤לֶךְ וְהָמָן֙ יָשְׁב֣וּ לִשְׁתּ֔וֹת וְהָעִ֥יר שׁוּשָׁ֖ן נָבֽוֹכָה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐσπεύδετο δὲ τὸ πρᾶγμα καὶ εἰς Σουσάν· ὁ δὲ βασιλεὺς καὶ Ἁμὰν ἐκωθωνίζοντο, ἐταράσσετο δὲ ἡ πόλις.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The posts went forth in haste by the king's commandment, and the decree was given out in Shushan the palace. And the king and Haman sat down to drink; but the city of Shushan was perplexed.
					\item[NIV] The couriers went out, spurred on by the king’s command, and the edict was issued in the citadel of Susa. The king and Haman sat down to drink, but the city of Susa was bewildered.
					\item[NRSV] The couriers went quickly by order of the king, and the decree was issued in the citadel of Susa. The king and Haman sat down to drink; but the city of Susa was thrown into confusion.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Esther 4} % *ESTHER-4
	% -----
		\label{ESTHER-4}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:1} % *ESTHER-4-1
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-1}

			When Mordecai perceived all that was done, Mordecai rent his clothes, and put on sackcloth with ashes, and went out into the midst of the city, and cried with a loud and a bitter cry;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמָרְדֳּכַ֗י יָדַע֙ אֶת־כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר נַעֲשָׂ֔ה וַיִּקְרַ֤ע מָרְדֳּכַי֙ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֔יו וַיִּלְבַּ֥שׁ שַׂ֖ק וָאֵ֑פֶר וַיֵּצֵא֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעִ֔יר וַיִּזְעַ֛ק זְעָקָ֥ה גְדֹלָ֖ה וּמָרָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Ὁ δὲ Μαρδοχαῖος ἐπιγνοὺς τὸ συντελούμενον διέρρηξεν τὰ ἱμάτια ἑαυτοῦ, καὶ ἐνεδύσατο σάκκον καὶ κατεπάσατο σποδόν, καὶ ἐκπηδήσας διὰ τῆς πλατείας τῆς πόλεως ἐβόα φωνῇ μεγάλῃ Αἴρεται ἔθνος μηδὲν ἠδικηκός.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now when Mordecai knew all that was done, Mordecai rent his clothes, and put on sackcloth with ashes, and went out into the midst of the city, and cried with a loud and a bitter cry;
					\item[NIV] When Mordecai learned of all that had been done, he tore his clothes, put on sackcloth and ashes, and went out into the city, wailing loudly and bitterly.
					\item[NRSV] When Mordecai learned all that had been done, Mordecai tore his clothes and put on sackcloth and ashes, and went through the city, wailing with a loud and bitter cry;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:2} % *ESTHER-4-2
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-2}

			And came even before the king’s gate: for none might enter into the king’s gate clothed with sackcloth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּב֕וֹא עַ֖ד לִפְנֵ֣י שַֽׁעַר־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ כִּ֣י אֵ֥ין לָב֛וֹא אֶל־שַׁ֥עַר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בִּלְב֥וּשׁ שָֽׂק׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἦλθεν ἕως τῆς πύλης τοῦ βασιλέως, καὶ ἔστη· οὐ γὰρ ἦν ἐξὸν αὐτῷ εἰσελθεῖν εἰς τὴν αὐλὴν σάκκον ἔχοντι καὶ σποδόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and he came even before the king's gate: for none might enter within the king's gate clothed with sackcloth.
					\item[NIV] But he went only as far as the king’s gate, because no one clothed in sackcloth was allowed to enter it.
					\item[NRSV] he went up to the entrance of the king’s gate, for no one might enter the king’s gate clothed with sackcloth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:3} % *ESTHER-4-3
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-3}

			And in every province, whithersoever the king’s commandment and his decree came, there was great mourning among the Jews, and fasting, and weeping, and wailing; and many lay in sackcloth and ashes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבְכָל־מְדִינָ֣ה וּמְדִינָ֗ה מְקוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר דְּבַר־הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ וְדָתוֹ֙ מַגִּ֔יעַ אֵ֤בֶל גָּדוֹל֙ לַיְּהוּדִ֔ים וְצ֥וֹם וּבְכִ֖י וּמִסְפֵּ֑ד שַׂ֣ק וָאֵ֔פֶר יֻצַּ֖ע לָֽרַבִּֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐν πάσῃ χώρᾳ οὗ ἐξετίθετο τὰ γράμματα κραυγὴ καὶ κοπετὸς καὶ πένθος μέγα τοῖς Ἰουδαίοις, σάκκον καὶ σποδὸν ἔστρωσαν ἑαυτοῖς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And in every province, whithersoever the king's commandment and his decree came, there was great mourning among the Jews, and fasting, and weeping, and wailing; and many lay in sackcloth and ashes.
					\item[NIV] In every province to which the edict and order of the king came, there was great mourning among the Jews, with fasting, weeping and wailing. Many lay in sackcloth and ashes.
					\item[NRSV] In every province, wherever the king’s command and his decree came, there was great mourning among the Jews, with fasting and weeping and lamenting, and most of them lay in sackcloth and ashes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:4} % *ESTHER-4-4
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-4}

			So Esther’s maids and her chamberlains came and told it her. Then was the queen exceedingly grieved; and she sent raiment to clothe Mordecai, and to take away his sackcloth from him: but he received it not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַ֠תָּבוֹאינָה נַעֲר֨וֹת אֶסְתֵּ֤ר וְסָרִיסֶ֙יהָ֙ וַיַּגִּ֣ידוּ לָ֔הּ וַתִּתְחַלְחַ֥ל הַמַּלְכָּ֖ה מְאֹ֑ד וַתִּשְׁלַ֨ח בְּגָדִ֜ים לְהַלְבִּ֣ישׁ אֶֽת־מָרְדֳּכַ֗י וּלְהָסִ֥יר שַׂקּ֛וֹ מֵעָלָ֖יו וְלֹ֥א קִבֵּֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἰσῆλθον αἱ ἅβραι καὶ οἱ εὐνοῦχοι τῆς βασιλίσσης καὶ ἀνήγγειλαν αὐτῇ· καὶ ἐταράχθη ἀκούσασα τὸ γεγονός, καὶ ἀπέστειλεν στολίσαι τὸν Μαρδοχαῖον καὶ ἀφελέσθαι αὐτοῦ τὸν σάκκον· ὁ δὲ οὐκ ἐπείσθη.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Esther's maidens and her chamberlains came and told it her; and the queen was exceedingly grieved: and she sent raiment to clothe Mordecai, and to take his sackcloth from off him; but he received it not.
					\item[NIV] When Esther’s eunuchs and female attendants came and told her about Mordecai, she was in great distress. She sent clothes for him to put on instead of his sackcloth, but he would not accept them.
					\item[NRSV] When Esther’s maids and her eunuchs came and told her, the queen was deeply distressed; she sent garments to clothe Mordecai, so that he might take off his sackcloth; but he would not accept them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:5} % *ESTHER-4-5
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-5}

			Then called Esther for Hatach, one of the king’s chamberlains, whom he had appointed to attend upon her, and gave him a commandment to Mordecai, to know what it was, and why it was.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּקְרָא֩ אֶסְתֵּ֨ר לַהֲתָ֜ךְ מִסָּרִיסֵ֤י הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֶעֱמִ֣יד לְפָנֶ֔יהָ וַתְּצַוֵּ֖הוּ עַֽל־מָרְדֳּכָ֑י לָדַ֥עַת מַה־זֶּ֖ה וְעַל־מַה־זֶּֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἡ δὲ Ἐσθὴρ προσεκαλέσατο Ἁχραθαῖον τὸν εὐνοῦχον αὐτῆς, ὃς παριστήκει αὐτῇ, καὶ ἀπέστειλεν μαθεῖν αὐτῇ παρὰ τοῦ Μαρδοχαίου τὸ ἀκριβές.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then called Esther for Hathach, one of the king's chamberlains, whom he had appointed to attend upon her, and charged him to go to Mordecai, to know what this was, and why it was.
					\item[NIV] Then Esther summoned Hathak, one of the king’s eunuchs assigned to attend her, and ordered him to find out what was troubling Mordecai and why.
					\item[NRSV] Then Esther called for Hathach, one of the king’s eunuchs, who had been appointed to attend her, and ordered him to go to Mordecai to learn what was happening and why.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:6} % *ESTHER-4-6
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-6}

			So Hatach went forth to Mordecai unto the street of the city, which was before the king’s gate.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּצֵ֥א הֲתָ֖ךְ אֶֽל־מָרְדֳּכָ֑י אֶל־רְח֣וֹב הָעִ֔יר אֲשֶׁ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י שַֽׁעַר־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Hathach went forth to Mordecai unto the broad place of the city, which was before the king's gate.
					\item[NIV] So Hathak went out to Mordecai in the open square of the city in front of the king’s gate.
					\item[NRSV] Hathach went out to Mordecai in the open square of the city in front of the king’s gate,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:7} % *ESTHER-4-7
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-7}

			And Mordecai told him of all that had happened unto him, and of the sum of the money that Haman had promised to pay to the king’s treasuries for the Jews, to destroy them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּגֶּד־ל֣וֹ מָרְדֳּכַ֔י אֵ֖ת כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר קָרָ֑הוּ וְאֵ֣ת ׀ פָּרָשַׁ֣ת הַכֶּ֗סֶף אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָמַ֤ר הָמָן֙ לִ֠שְׁקוֹל עַל־גִּנְזֵ֥י הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ בַּיְּהוּדִ֖ים לְאַבְּדָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ δὲ Μαρδοχαῖος ὑπέδειξεν αὐτῷ τὸ γεγονὸς καὶ τὴν ἐπαγγελίαν ἥν ἐπηγγείλατο Ἁμὰν τῷ βασιλεῖ εἰς τὴν γάζαν μυρίων ταλάντων, ἵνα ἀπολέσῃ τοὺς Ἰουδαίους.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Mordecai told him of all that had happened unto him, and the exact sum of the money that Haman had promised to pay to the king's treasuries for the Jews, to destroy them.
					\item[NIV] Mordecai told him everything that had happened to him, including the exact amount of money Haman had promised to pay into the royal treasury for the destruction of the Jews.
					\item[NRSV] and Mordecai told him all that had happened to him, and the exact sum of money that Haman had promised to pay into the king’s treasuries for the destruction of the Jews.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:8} % *ESTHER-4-8
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-8}

			Also he gave him the copy of the writing of the decree that was given at Shushan to destroy them, to shew it unto Esther, and to declare it unto her, and to charge her that she should go in unto the king, to make supplication unto him, and to make request before him for her people.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֶת־פַּתְשֶׁ֣גֶן כְּתָֽב־הַ֠דָּת אֲשֶׁר־נִתַּ֨ן בְּשׁוּשָׁ֤ן לְהַשְׁמִידָם֙ נָ֣תַן ל֔וֹ לְהַרְא֥וֹת אֶת־אֶסְתֵּ֖ר וּלְהַגִּ֣יד לָ֑הּ וּלְצַוּ֣וֹת עָלֶ֗יהָ לָב֨וֹא אֶל־הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ לְהִֽתְחַנֶּן־ל֛וֹ וּלְבַקֵּ֥שׁ מִלְּפָנָ֖יו עַל־עַמָּֽהּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ τὸ ἀντίγραφον τὸ ἐν Σούσοις ἐκτεθὲν ὑπὲρ τοῦ ἀπολέσθαι αὐτοὺς ἔδωκεν αὐτῷ δεῖξαι τῇ Ἐσθήρ· καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ ἐντείλασθαι αὐτῇ εἰσελθούσῃ παραιτήσασθαι τὸν βασιλέα καὶ ἀξιῶσαι αὐτὸν περὶ τοῦ λαοῦ, μνησθεῖσα ἡμερῶν ταπεινώσεώς σου ὡς ἐτράφης ἐν χειρί μου, διότι Ἁμὰν ὁ δευτερεύων τῷ βασιλεῖ ἐλάλησεν καθ᾽ ἡμῶν εἰς θάνατον· ἐπικάλεσαι τὸν κύριον καὶ λὰλησον τῷ βασιλεῖ περὶ ἡμῶν καὶ ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἐκ θανάτου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Also he gave him the copy of the writing of the decree that was given out in Shushan to destroy them, to show it unto Esther, and to declare it unto her, and to charge her that she should go in unto the king, to make supplication unto him, and to make request before him, for her people.
					\item[NIV] He also gave him a copy of the text of the edict for their annihilation, which had been published in Susa, to show to Esther and explain it to her, and he told him to instruct her to go into the king’s presence to beg for mercy and plead with him for her people.
					\item[NRSV] Mordecai also gave him a copy of the written decree issued in Susa for their destruction, that he might show it to Esther, explain it to her, and charge her to go to the king to make supplication to him and entreat him for her people.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-9}

			And Hatach came and told Esther the words of Mordecai.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּב֖וֹא הֲתָ֑ךְ וַיַּגֵּ֣ד לְאֶסְתֵּ֔ר אֵ֖ת דִּבְרֵ֥י מָרְדֳּכָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἰσελθὼν δὲ ὁ Ἁχραθαῖος ἐλάλησεν αὐτῇ πάντας τοὺς λόγους τούτους.}

					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ Ἐσθὴρ πρὸς Ἁχραθαῖον Πορεύθητι πρὸς Μαρδοχαῖον καὶ εἰπὸν}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:11} % *ESTHER-4-11
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-11}

			All the king’s servants, and the people of the king’s provinces, do know, that whosoever, whether man or woman, shall come unto the king into the inner court, who is not called, there is one law of his to put him to death, except such to whom the king shall hold out the golden sceptre, that he may live: but I have not been called to come in unto the king these thirty days.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־עַבְדֵ֣י הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ וְעַם־מְדִינ֨וֹת הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ יֽוֹדְעִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר כָּל־אִ֣ישׁ וְאִשָּׁ֡ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָבֽוֹא־אֶל־הַמֶּלֶךְ֩ אֶל־הֶחָצֵ֨ר הַפְּנִימִ֜ית אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־יִקָּרֵ֗א אַחַ֤ת דָּתוֹ֙ לְהָמִ֔ית לְ֠בַד מֵאֲשֶׁ֨ר יֽוֹשִׁיט־ל֥וֹ הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ אֶת־שַׁרְבִ֥יט הַזָּהָ֖ב וְחָיָ֑ה וַאֲנִ֗י לֹ֤א נִקְרֵ֙אתי֙ לָב֣וֹא אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ זֶ֖ה שְׁלוֹשִׁ֥ים יֽוֹם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι Τὰ ἔθνη πάντα τῆς βασιλείας γινώσκει, ὅτι πᾶς ἄνθρωπος ἢ γυνὴ ὃς εἰσελεύσεται πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα εἰς τὴν αὐλὴν τὴν ἐσωτέραν ἄκλητος, οὐκ ἔστιν αὐτῷ σωτηρία· πλὴν ᾧ ἐκτείνει ὁ βασιλεὺς τὴν χρυσῆν ῥάβδον, οὗτος σωθήσεται· κἀγὼ οὐ κέκλημαι εἰσελθεῖν πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα, εἰσὶν αὗται ἡμέραι τριάκοντα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All the king's servants, and the people of the king's provinces, do know, that whosoever, whether man or woman, shall come unto the king into the inner court, who is not called, there is one law for him, that he be put to death, except those to whom the king shall hold out the golden sceptre, that he may live: but I have not been called to come in unto the king these thirty days.
					\item[NIV] “All the king’s officials and the people of the royal provinces know that for any man or woman who approaches the king in the inner court without being summoned the king has but one law: that they be put to death unless the king extends the gold scepter to them and spares their lives. But thirty days have passed since I was called to go to the king.”
					\item[NRSV] “All the king’s servants and the people of the king’s provinces know that if any man or woman goes to the king inside the inner court without being called, there is but one law—all alike are to be put to death. Only if the king holds out the golden scepter to someone, may that person live. I myself have not been called to come in to the king for thirty days.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπήγγειλεν Ἁχραθαῖος Μαρδοχαίῳ πάντας τοὺς λόγους Ἐσθήρ.}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:13} % *ESTHER-4-13
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-13}

			Then Mordecai commanded to answer Esther, Think not with thyself that thou shalt escape in the king’s house, more than all the Jews.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר מָרְדֳּכַ֖י לְהָשִׁ֣יב אֶל־אֶסְתֵּ֑ר אַל־תְּדַמִּ֣י בְנַפְשֵׁ֔ךְ לְהִמָּלֵ֥ט בֵּית־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ מִכָּל־הַיְּהוּדִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Μαρδοχαῖος πρὸς Ἁχραθαῖον Πορεύθητι καὶ εἰπὸν αὐτῇ Ἐσθήρ, μὴ εἴπῃς σεαυτῇ ὅτι σωθήσῃ μόνη ἐν τῇ βασιλείᾳ παρὰ πάντας τοὺς Ἰουδαίους·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Mordecai bade them return answer unto Esther, Think not with thyself that thou shalt escape in the king's house, more than all the Jews.
					\item[NIV] he sent back this answer: “Do not think that because you are in the king’s house you alone of all the Jews will escape.
					\item[NRSV] Mordecai told them to reply to Esther, “Do not think that in the king’s palace you will escape any more than all the other Jews.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:14} % *ESTHER-4-14
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-14}

			For if thou altogether holdest thy peace at this time, then shall there enlargement and deliverance arise to the Jews from another place; but thou and thy father’s house shall be destroyed: and who knoweth whether thou art come to the kingdom for such a time as this?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י אִם־הַחֲרֵ֣שׁ תַּחֲרִישִׁי֮ בָּעֵ֣ת הַזֹּאת֒ רֶ֣וַח וְהַצָּלָ֞ה יַעֲמ֤וֹד לַיְּהוּדִים֙ מִמָּק֣וֹם אַחֵ֔ר וְאַ֥תְּ וּבֵית־אָבִ֖יךְ תֹּאבֵ֑דוּ וּמִ֣י יוֹדֵ֔עַ אִם־לְעֵ֣ת כָּזֹ֔את הִגַּ֖עַתְּ לַמַּלְכֽוּת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὡς ὅτι ἐὰν παρακούσῃς ἐν τούτῳ τῷ καιρῷ, ἄλλοθεν βοήθεια καὶ σκέπη ἔσται τοῖς Ἰουδαίοις· σὺ δὲ καὶ ὁ οἶκος τοῦ πατρός σου ἀπολεῖσθε. καὶ τίς οἶδεν εἰ εἰς τὸν καιρὸν τοῦτον ἐβασίλευσας;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For if thou altogether holdest thy peace at this time, then will relief and deliverance arise to the Jews from another place, but thou and thy father's house will perish: and who knoweth whether thou art not come to the kingdom for such a time as this?
					\item[NIV] For if you remain silent at this time, relief and deliverance for the Jews will arise from another place, but you and your father’s family will perish. And who knows but that you have come to your royal position for such a time as this?”
					\item[NRSV] For if you keep silence at such a time as this, relief and deliverance will rise for the Jews from another quarter, but you and your father’s family will perish. Who knows? Perhaps you have come to royal dignity for just such a time as this.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---

			Then Esther bade them return Mordecai this answer,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֹּ֥אמֶר אֶסְתֵּ֖ר לְהָשִׁ֥יב אֶֽל־מָרְדֳּכָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐξαπέστειλεν Ἐσθὴρ τὸν ἥκοντα πρὸς αὐτὴν πρὸς Μαρδοχαῖον λέγουσα}

					% % \item[NETS]

					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Esther bade them return answer unto Mordecai,
					\item[NIV] Then Esther sent this reply to Mordecai:
					\item[NRSV] Then Esther said in reply to Mordecai,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:16} % *ESTHER-4-16
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-16}

			Go, gather together all the Jews that are present in Shushan, and fast ye for me, and neither eat nor drink three days, night or day: I also and my maidens will fast likewise; and so will I go in unto the king, which is not according to the law: and if I perish, I perish.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵךְ֩ כְּנ֨וֹס אֶת־כָּל־הַיְּהוּדִ֜ים הַֽנִּמְצְאִ֣ים בְּשׁוּשָׁ֗ן וְצ֣וּמוּ עָ֠לַי וְאַל־תֹּאכְל֨וּ וְאַל־תִּשְׁתּ֜וּ שְׁלֹ֤שֶׁת יָמִים֙ לַ֣יְלָה וָי֔וֹם גַּם־אֲנִ֥י וְנַעֲרֹתַ֖י אָצ֣וּם כֵּ֑ן וּבְכֵ֞ן אָב֤וֹא אֶל־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־כַדָּ֔ת וְכַאֲשֶׁ֥ר אָבַ֖דְתִּי אָבָֽדְתִּי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Βαδίσας ἐκκλησίασον τοὺς Ἰουδαίους τοὺς ἐν Σούσοις, καὶ νηστεύσατε ἐπ᾽ ἐμοί, καὶ μὴ φάγητε μηδὲ πίητε ἐπὶ ἡμέρας τρεῖς νύκτα καὶ ἡμέραν· κἀγὼ δὲ καὶ αἱ ἅβραι μου ἀσιτήσομεν· καὶ τότε εἰσελεύσομαι πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα παρὰ τὸν νόμον, ἐὰν καὶ ἀπολέσθαι με ᾖ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Go, gather together all the Jews that are present in Shushan, and fast ye for me, and neither eat nor drink three days, night or day: I also and my maidens will fast in like manner; and so will I go in unto the king, which is not according to the law: and if I perish, I perish.
					\item[NIV] “Go, gather together all the Jews who are in Susa, and fast for me. Do not eat or drink for three days, night or day. I and my attendants will fast as you do. When this is done, I will go to the king, even though it is against the law. And if I perish, I perish.”
					\item[NRSV] “Go, gather all the Jews to be found in Susa, and hold a fast on my behalf, and neither eat nor drink for three days, night or day. I and my maids will also fast as you do. After that I will go to the king, though it is against the law; and if I perish, I perish.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 4:17} % *ESTHER-4-17
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-4-17}

			So Mordecai went his way, and did according to all that Esther had commanded him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֖ר מָרְדֳּכָ֑י וַיַּ֕עַשׂ כְּכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוְּתָ֥ה עָלָ֖יו אֶסְתֵּֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ βαδίσας Μαρδοχαῖος ἐποίησεν ὅσα ἐνετείλατο αὐτῷ Ἐσθήρ· [1] καὶ ἐδεήθη Κυρίου, μνημονεύων πάντα τὰ ἔργα Κυρίου, [2] καὶ εἶπεν Κύριε, κύριε βασιλεῦ πάντων κρατῶν, ὅτι ἐν ἐξουσίᾳ σου τὸ πᾶν ἐστιν, καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν ὁ ἀντιδοξῶν σοι ἐν τῷ θέλειν σε σῶσαι τὸν Ἰσραήλ· [3] ὅτι σὺ ἐποίησας τὸν οὐρανὸν καὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ πᾶν θαυμαζόμενον ἐν τῇ ὑπ᾽ οὐρανόν, [4] καὶ κύριος εἶ πάντων, καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν ὃς ἀντιτάξεταί σοι τῷ κυρίῳ· [5] σὺ πάντα γινώσκεις, σὺ οἶδας, Κύριε, ὅτι οὐκ ἐν ὕβρει οὐδὲ ἐν ὑπερηφανίᾳ οὐδὲ ἐν φιλοδοξίᾳ ἐποίησα τοῦτο, τὸ μὴ προσκυνεῖν τὸν ὑπερήφανον Ἁμάν. [6] ὅτι ηὐδόκουν φιλεῖν πέλματα ποδῶν αὐτοῦ πρὸς σωτηρίαν Ἰσραήλ, [7] ἀλλὰ ἐποίησα τοῦτο ἵνα μὴ θῶ δόξαν ἀνθρώπου ὑπεράνω δόξης θεοῦ· καὶ οὐ προσκυνήσω οὐδένα πλὴν σοῦ τοῦ κυρίου μου, καὶ οὐ ποιήσω αὐτὰ ἐν ὑπερηφανίᾳ. [8] καὶ νῦν, Κύριε ὁ θεὸς ὁ βασιλεὺς ὁ θεὸς Ἀβραάμ, φεῖσαι τοῦ λαοῦ σου, ὅτι ἐπιβλέπουσιν ἡμῖν εἰς καταφθορὰν καὶ ἐπεθύμησαν ἀπολέσαι τὴν ἐξ ἀρχῆς κληρονομίαν σου· [9] μὴ ὑπερίδῃς τὴν μερίδα σου ἣν σεαυτῷ ἐλυτρώσω ἐκ γῆς Αἰγύπτου· [10] ἐπάκουσον τῆς δεήσεώς μου καὶ ἱλάσθητι τῷ κλήρῳ σου, καὶ στρέψον τὸ πένθος ἡμῶν εἰς εὐωχίαν, ἵνα ζῶντες ὑμνῶμέν σου τὸ ὄνομα, Κύριε, καὶ μὴ ἀφανίσῃς στόμα αἰνούντων σοι. [11] καὶ πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ ἐκέκραξαν ἐξ ἰσχύος αὐτῶν, ὅτι θάνατος αὐτῶν ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς αὐτῶν. [12] Καὶ Ἐσθὴρ ἡ βασίλισσα κατέφυγεν ἐπὶ τὸν κύριον ἐν ἀγῶνι θανάτον κατειλημμένη, [13] καὶ ἀφελομένη τὰ ἱμάτια τῆς δόξης αὐτῆς ἐνεδύσατο ἱμάτια στενοχωρίας καὶ πένθους, καὶ ἀντὶ τῶν ὑπερηφάνων ἡδυσμάτων σποδοῦ καὶ κοπριῶν ἔπλησεν τὴν κεφαλήν· καὶ τὸ σῶμα ἐταπείνωσεν σφόδρα, καὶ πάντα τόπον κόσμου ἀγαλλιάματος αὐτῆς ἔπλησε στρεπτῶν τριχῶν αὐτῆς· [14] καὶ ἐδεῖτο Κυρίου θεοῦ Ἰσραὴλ καὶ εἶπεν Κύριέ μου, ὁ βασιλεὺς ἡμῶν, σὺ εἶ μόνος· βοήθησόν μοι τῇ μόνῃ καὶ μὴ ἐχούσῃ βοηθὸν εἰ μὴ σέ, [15] ὅτι κίνδυνός μου ἐν χειρί μου. [16] ἐγὼ ἤκουον ἐκ γενετῆς μου ἐν φυλῇ πατριᾶς μου, ὅτι σύ, Κύριε, ἔλαβες τὸν Ἰσραὴλ ἐκ πάντων τῶν ἐθνῶν καὶ τοὺς πατέρας ἡμῶν ἐκ πάντων τῶν προγόνων αὐτῶν εἰς κληρονομίαν αἰώνιον, καὶ ἐποίησας αὐτοῖς ὅσα ἐλάλησας. [17] καὶ νῦν ἡμάρτομεν ἐνώπιόν σου, καὶ παρέδωκας ἡμᾶς εἰς χεῖρας τῶν ἐχθρῶν ἡμῶν· [18] ἀνθ᾽ ὧν ἐδοξάσαμεν τοὺς θεοὺς αὐτῶν. δίκαιος εἶ, Κύριε· [19] καὶ νῦν οὐχ ἱκανώθησαν ἐν πικρασμῷ δουλείας ἡμῶν, ἀλλὰ ἔθηκαν τὰς χεῖρας αὐτῶν, [20] ἐξᾶραι ὁρισμὸν στόματός σου καὶ ἀφανίσαι κληρονομίαν σου, καὶ ἐμφράξαι στόμα αἰνούντων σοι καὶ σβέσαι δόξαν οἴκου σου καὶ θυσιαστήριόν σου, [21] καὶ ἀνοῖξαι στόμα ἐθνῶν εἰς ἀρετὰς ματαίων καὶ θαυμασθῆναι βασιλέα σάρκινον εἰς αἰῶνα. [22] μὴ παραδῷς, Κύριε, τὸ σκῆπτρόν σου τοῖς μὴ οὖσιν, καὶ μὴ καταγελασάτωσαν ἐν τῇ πτώσει ἡμῶν, ἀλλὰ στρέψον τὴν βουλὴν αὐτῶν ἐπ᾽ αὐτούς· τὸν δὲ ἀρξάμενον ἐφ᾽ ἡμᾶς παραδιγμάτισον. [23] μνήσθητι, Κύριε, γνώσθητι ἐν καιρῷ θλίψεως ἡμῶν, καὶ ἐμὲ θάρσυνον, βασιλεῦ τῶν θεῶν καὶ πάσης ἀρχῆς ἐπικρατῶν· [24] δὸς λόγον εὔρυθμον εἰς τὸ στόμα μου ἐνώπιον τοῦ λέοντος, καὶ μετάθες τὴν καρδίαν αὐτοῦ εἰς μῖσος τοῦ πολεμοῦντος ἡμᾶς, εἰς συντέλειαν αὐτοῦ καὶ τῶν ὁμονοούντων αὐτῷ· [25] ἡμᾶς δὲ ῥῦσαι ἐν χειρί σου, καὶ βοήθησόν μοι τῇ μόνῃ καὶ μὴ ἐχούσῃ εἰ μὴ σέ, Κύριε. πάντων γνῶσιν ἔχεις, [26] καὶ οἶδας ὅτι ἐμίσησα δόξαν ἀνόμων, καὶ βδελύσσομαι κοίτην ἀπεριτμήτων καὶ παντὸς ἀλλοτρίου. [27] σὺ οἶδας τὴν ἀνάγκην μου, ὅτι βδελύσσομαι τὸ σημεῖον τῆς ὑπερηφανίας μου ὅ ἐστιν ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς μου ἐν ἡμέραις ὁπτασίας μου· βδελύσσομαι αὐτὸ ὡς ῥάκος καταμηνίων, καὶ οὐ φορῶ αὐτὸ ἐν ἡμέραις ἡσυχίας μου. [28] καὶ οὐκ ἔφαγεν ἡ δούλη σου τράπεζαν Ἁμάν, καὶ οὐκ ἐδόξασα συμπόσιον βασιλέως, οὐδὲ ἔπιον οἶνον σπονδῶν· [29] καὶ οὐκ ηὐφράνθη ἡ δούλη σου ἀφ᾽ ἡμέρας μεταβολῆς μου μέχρι νῦν πλὴν ἐπὶ σοί, Κύριε ὁ θεὸς Ἁβραάμ. [30] ὁ θεὸς ὁ ἰσχύων ἐπὶ πάντας, εἰσάκουσον φωνὴν ἀπηλπισμένων καὶ ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἐκ χειρὸς τῶν πονηρευομένων, καὶ ῥῦσαί με ἐκ τοῦ φόβου μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Mordecai went his way, and did according to all that Esther had commanded him.
					\item[NIV] So Mordecai went away and carried out all of Esther’s instructions.
					\item[NRSV] Mordecai then went away and did everything as Esther had ordered him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Esther 5} % *ESTHER-5
	% -----
		\label{ESTHER-5}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:1} % *ESTHER-5-1
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-1}

			Now it came to pass on the third day, that Esther put on her royal apparel, and stood in the inner court of the king’s house, over against the king’s house: and the king sat upon his royal throne in the royal house, over against the gate of the house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י ׀ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֗י וַתִּלְבַּ֤שׁ אֶסְתֵּר֙ מַלְכ֔וּת וַֽתַּעֲמֹ֞ד בַּחֲצַ֤ר בֵּית־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַפְּנִימִ֔ית נֹ֖כַח בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְ֠הַמֶּלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁ֞ב עַל־כִּסֵּ֤א מַלְכוּתוֹ֙ בְּבֵ֣ית הַמַּלְכ֔וּת נֹ֖כַח פֶּ֥תַח הַבָּֽיִת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{[1] Καὶ ἐγενήθη ἐν τῇ ἡμέρᾳ τῇ τρίτῃ ὡς ἐπαύσατο προσευχομένη, ἐξεδύσατο τὰ ἱμάτια τῆς θεραπείας καὶ περιεβάλετο τὴν δόξαν αὐτῆς· [2] καὶ γενηθεῖσα ἐπιφανής, ἐπικαλεσαμένη τὸν πάντων ἐπόπτην θεὸν καὶ σωτῆρα, παρέλαβεν τὰς δύο ἅβρας, [3] καὶ τῇ μὲν μιᾷ ἐπηρείδετο ὡς τρυφερευομένη, [4] ἡ δὲ ἑτέρα ἐπηκολούθει κουφίζουσα τὴν ἔνδυσιν αὐτῆς· [5] καὶ αὐτὴ ἐρυθριῶσα ἀκμῇ κάλλους αὐτῆς, καὶ τὸ πρόσωπον αὐτῆς ἱλαρὸν ὡς προσφιλές, ἡ δὲ καρδία αὐτῆς ἀπεστενωμένη ἀπὸ τοῦ φόβου. [6] καὶ εἰσελθοῦσα πάσας τὰς θύρας κατέστη ἐνώπιον τοῦ βασιλέως· καὶ αὐτὸς ἐκάθητο ἐπὶ τοῦ θρόνου τῆς βασιλείας αὐτοῦ, καὶ πᾶσαν στολὴν τῆς ἐπιφανείας αὐτοῦ ἐνεδεδύκει, ὅλος διὰ χρυσοῦ καὶ λίθων πολυτελῶν, καὶ ἦν φοβερὸς σφόδρα. [7] καὶ ἄρας τὸ πρόσωπον αὐτοῦ πεπυρωμένον δόξῃ ἐν ἀκμῇ θυμοῦ ἔβλεψεν· καὶ ἔπεσεν ἡ βασίλισσα καὶ μετέβαλεν τὸ χρῶμα αὐτῆς ἐν ἐκλύσει, καὶ κατεπέκυψεν ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν τῆς ἅβρας τῆς προπορευομένης. [8] καὶ μετέβαλεν ὁ θεὸς τὸ πνεῦμα τοῦ βασιλέως εἰς πραύτητα, καὶ ἀγωνιάσας ἀνεπήδησεν ἀπὸ τοῦ θρόνου αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἀνέλαβεν αὐτὴν ἐπὶ τὰς ἀγκάλας αὐτοῦ μέχρις οὗ κατέστη, καὶ παρεκάλει αὐτὴν λόγοις εἰρηνικοῖς [9] καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῇ Τί ἐστιν, Ἐσθήρ; ἐγὼ ὁ ἀδελφός σου, θάρσει, [10] οὐ μὴ ἀποθάνῃς· ὅτι κοινὸν τὸ πρόσταγμα ἡμῶν ἐστίν· [11] πρόσελθε.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now it came to pass on the third day, that Esther put on her royal apparel, and stood in the inner court of the king's house, over against the king's house: and the king sat upon his royal throne in the royal house, over against the entrance of the house.
					\item[NIV] On the third day Esther put on her royal robes and stood in the inner court of the palace, in front of the king’s hall. The king was sitting on his royal throne in the hall, facing the entrance.
					\item[NRSV] On the third day Esther put on her royal robes and stood in the inner court of the king's palace, opposite the king's hall. The king was sitting on his royal throne inside the palace opposite the entrance to the palace.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:2} % *ESTHER-5-2
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-2}

			And it was so, when the king saw Esther the queen standing in the court, that she obtained favour in his sight: and the king held out to Esther the golden sceptre that was in his hand. So Esther drew near, and touched the top of the sceptre.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִי֩ כִרְא֨וֹת הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אֶת־אֶסְתֵּ֣ר הַמַּלְכָּ֗ה עֹמֶ֙דֶת֙ בֶּֽחָצֵ֔ר נָשְׂאָ֥ה חֵ֖ן בְּעֵינָ֑יו וַיּ֨וֹשֶׁט הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ לְאֶסְתֵּ֗ר אֶת־שַׁרְבִ֤יט הַזָּהָב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּיָד֔וֹ וַתִּקְרַ֣ב אֶסְתֵּ֔ר וַתִּגַּ֖ע בְּרֹ֥אשׁ הַשַּׁרְבִֽיט׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{[12] καὶ ἄρας τὴν χρυσῆν ῥάβδον ἐπέθηκεν ἐπὶ τὸν τράχηλον αὐτῆς, καὶ ἠσπάσατο αὐτὴν καὶ εἶπεν Λάλησόν μοι. [13] καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ Εἶδόν σε, κύριε, ὡς ἄγγελον θεοῦ, καὶ ἐταράχθη ἡ καρδία μου ἀπὸ φόβου τῆς δόξης σου. [14] ὅτι θαυμαστὸς εἶ, κύριε, καὶ τὸ πρόσωπόν σου χαρίτων μεστόν. [15] ἐν δὲ τῷ διαλέγεσθαι αὐτὴν ἔπεσεν ἀπὸ ἐκλύσεως· [16] καὶ ὁ βασιλεὺς ἐταράσσετο, καὶ πᾶσα ἡ θεραπεία αὐτοῦ παρεκάλει αὐτήν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it was so, when the king saw Esther the queen standing in the court, that she obtained favor in his sight; and the king held out to Esther the golden sceptre that was in his hand. So Esther drew near, and touched the top of the sceptre.
					\item[NIV] When he saw Queen Esther standing in the court, he was pleased with her and held out to her the gold scepter that was in his hand. So Esther approached and touched the tip of the scepter.
					\item[NRSV] As soon as the king saw Queen Esther standing in the court, she won his favor and he held out to her the golden scepter that was in his hand. Then Esther approached and touched the top of the scepter.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:3} % *ESTHER-5-3
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-3}

			Then said the king unto her, What wilt thou, queen Esther? and what is thy request? it shall be even given thee to the half of the kingdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לָהּ֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מַה־לָּ֖ךְ אֶסְתֵּ֣ר הַמַּלְכָּ֑ה וּמַה־בַּקָּשָׁתֵ֛ךְ עַד־חֲצִ֥י הַמַּלְכ֖וּת וְיִנָּ֥תֵֽן לָֽךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεύς Τί θέλεις, Ἐσθήρ; καὶ τί σού ἐστιν τὸ ἀξίωμα; ἕως τοῦ ἡμίσους τῆς βασιλείας μου, καὶ ἔσται σοι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said the king unto her, What wilt thou, queen Esther? and what is thy request? it shall be given thee even to the half of the kingdom.
					\item[NIV] Then the king asked, “What is it, Queen Esther? What is your request? Even up to half the kingdom, it will be given you.”
					\item[NRSV] The king said to her, “What is it, Queen Esther? What is your request? It shall be given you, even to the half of my kingdom.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:4} % *ESTHER-5-4
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-4}

			And Esther answered, If it seem good unto the king, let the king and Haman come this day unto the banquet that I have prepared for him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֹּ֣אמֶר אֶסְתֵּ֔ר אִם־עַל־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ ט֑וֹב יָב֨וֹא הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ וְהָמָן֙ הַיּ֔וֹם אֶל־הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂ֥יתִי לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ Ἐσθήρ Ἡμέρα μου ἐπίσημος σήμερόν ἐστιν· εἰ οὖν δοκεῖ τῷ βασιλεῖ, ἐλθάτω καὶ αὐτὸς καὶ Ἁμὰν εἰς τὴν δοχὴν ἣν ποιήσω σήμερον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Esther said, If it seem good unto the king, let the king and Haman come this day unto the banquet that I have prepared for him.
					\item[NIV] “If it pleases the king,” replied Esther, “let the king, together with Haman, come today to a banquet I have prepared for him.”
					\item[NRSV] Then Esther said, “If it pleases the king, let the king and Haman come today to a banquet that I have prepared for the king.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:5} % *ESTHER-5-5
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-5}

			Then the king said, Cause Haman to make haste, that he may do as Esther hath said. So the king and Haman came to the banquet that Esther had prepared.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מַהֲרוּ֙ אֶת־הָמָ֔ן לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת אֶת־דְּבַ֣ר אֶסְתֵּ֑ר וַיָּבֹ֤א הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְהָמָ֔ן אֶל־הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשְׂתָ֥ה אֶסְתֵּֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεύς Κατασπεύσατε Ἁμὰν ὅπως ποιήσωμεν τὸν λόγον Ἐσθήρ· καὶ παραγίνονται ἀμφότεροι εἰς τὴν δοχὴν ἣν εἶπεν Ἐσθήρ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then the king said, Cause Haman to make haste, that it may be done as Esther hath said. So the king and Haman came to the banquet that Esther had prepared.
					\item[NIV] “Bring Haman at once,” the king said, “so that we may do what Esther asks.” So the king and Haman went to the banquet Esther had prepared.
					\item[NRSV] Then the king said, “Bring Haman quickly, so that we may do as Esther desires.” So the king and Haman came to the banquet that Esther had prepared.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:6} % *ESTHER-5-6
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-6}

			And the king said unto Esther at the banquet of wine, What is thy petition? and it shall be granted thee: and what is thy request? even to the half of the kingdom it shall be performed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ לְאֶסְתֵּר֙ בְּמִשְׁתֵּ֣ה הַיַּ֔יִן מַה־שְּׁאֵלָתֵ֖ךְ וְיִנָּ֣תֵֽן לָ֑ךְ וּמַה־בַּקָּשָׁתֵ֛ךְ עַד־חֲצִ֥י הַמַּלְכ֖וּת וְתֵעָֽשׂ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐν δὲ τῷ πότῳ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεὺς πρὸς Ἐσθήρ Τί ἐστιν, βασίλισσα Ἐσθήρ; καὶ ἔσται ὅσα ἀξιοῖς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said unto Esther at the banquet of wine, What is thy petition? and it shall be granted thee: and what is thy request? even to the half of the kingdom it shall be performed.
					\item[NIV] As they were drinking wine, the king again asked Esther, “Now what is your petition? It will be given you. And what is your request? Even up to half the kingdom, it will be granted.”
					\item[NRSV] While they were drinking wine, the king said to Esther, “What is your petition? It shall be granted you. And what is your request? Even to the half of my kingdom, it shall be fulfilled.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:7} % *ESTHER-5-7
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-7}

			Then answered Esther, and said, My petition and my request is;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתַּ֥עַן אֶסְתֵּ֖ר וַתֹּאמַ֑ר שְׁאֵלָתִ֖י וּבַקָּשָׁתִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Τὸ αἴτημά μου καὶ τὸ ἀξίωμα·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then answered Esther, and said, My petition and my request is:
					\item[NIV] Esther replied, “My petition and my request is this:
					\item[NRSV] Then Esther said, “This is my petition and request:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:8} % *ESTHER-5-8
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-8}

			If I have found favour in the sight of the king, and if it please the king to grant my petition, and to perform my request, let the king and Haman come to the banquet that I shall prepare for them, and I will do to morrow as the king hath said.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־מָצָ֨אתִי חֵ֜ן בְּעֵינֵ֣י הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וְאִם־עַל־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ ט֔וֹב לָתֵת֙ אֶת־שְׁאֵ֣לָתִ֔י וְלַעֲשׂ֖וֹת אֶת־בַּקָּשָׁתִ֑י יָב֧וֹא הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ וְהָמָ֗ן אֶל־הַמִּשְׁתֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶֽעֱשֶׂ֣ה לָהֶ֔ם וּמָחָ֥ר אֶֽעֱשֶׂ֖ה כִּדְבַ֥ר הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἰ εὗρον χάριν ἐνώπιον τοῦ βασιλέως, ἐλθάτω ὁ βασιλεὺς καὶ Ἁμὰν ἔτι τὴν αὔριον εἰς τὴν δοχὴν ἣν ποιήσω αὐτοῖς, καὶ αὔριον ποιήσω τὰ αὐτά.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If I have found favor in the sight of the king, and if it please the king to grant my petition, and to perform my request, let the king and Haman come to the banquet that I shall prepare for them, and I will do to-morrow as the king hath said.
					\item[NIV] If the king regards me with favor and if it pleases the king to grant my petition and fulfill my request, let the king and Haman come tomorrow to the banquet I will prepare for them. Then I will answer the king’s question.”
					\item[NRSV] If I have won the king’s favor, and if it pleases the king to grant my petition and fulfill my request, let the king and Haman come tomorrow to the banquet that I will prepare for them, and then I will do as the king has said.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:9} % *ESTHER-5-9
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-9}

			Then went Haman forth that day joyful and with a glad heart: but when Haman saw Mordecai in the king’s gate, that he stood not up, nor moved for him, he was full of indignation against Mordecai.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּצֵ֤א הָמָן֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא שָׂמֵ֖חַ וְט֣וֹב לֵ֑ב וְכִרְאוֹת֩ הָמָ֨ן אֶֽת־מָרְדֳּכַ֜י בְּשַׁ֣עַר הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וְלֹא־קָם֙ וְלֹא־זָ֣ע מִמֶּ֔נּוּ וַיִּמָּלֵ֥א הָמָ֛ן עַֽל־מָרְדֳּכַ֖י חֵמָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐξῆλθεν ὁ Ἁμὰν ἀπὸ τοῦ βασιλέως ὑπερχαρὴς εὐφραινόμενος· ἐν δὲ τῷ ἰδεῖν Ἁμὰν Μαρδοχαῖον τὸν Ἰουδαῖον ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ ἐθυμώθη σφόδρα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then went Haman forth that day joyful and glad of heart: but when Haman saw Mordecai in the king's gate, that he stood not up nor moved for him, he was filled with wrath against Mordecai.
					\item[NIV] Haman went out that day happy and in high spirits. But when he saw Mordecai at the king’s gate and observed that he neither rose nor showed fear in his presence, he was filled with rage against Mordecai.
					\item[NRSV] Haman went out that day happy and in good spirits. But when Haman saw Mordecai in the king’s gate, and observed that he neither rose nor trembled before him, he was infuriated with Mordecai;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:10} % *ESTHER-5-10
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-10}

			Nevertheless Haman refrained himself: and when he came home, he sent and called for his friends, and Zeresh his wife.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּתְאַפַּ֣ק הָמָ֔ן וַיָּב֖וֹא אֶל־בֵּית֑וֹ וַיִּשְׁלַ֛ח וַיָּבֵ֥א אֶת־אֹהֲבָ֖יו וְאֶת־זֶ֥רֶשׁ אִשְׁתּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἰσελθὼν εἰς τὰ ἴδια ἐκάλεσεν τοὺς φίλους καὶ Ζωσάραν τὴν γυναῖκα αὐτοῦ,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Nevertheless Haman refrained himself, and went home; and he sent and fetched his friends and Zeresh his wife.
					\item[NIV] Nevertheless, Haman restrained himself and went home. Calling together his friends and Zeresh, his wife,
					\item[NRSV] nevertheless Haman restrained himself and went home. Then he sent and called for his friends and his wife Zeresh,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:11} % *ESTHER-5-11
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-11}

			And Haman told them of the glory of his riches, and the multitude of his children, and all the things wherein the king had promoted him, and how he had advanced him above the princes and servants of the king.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְסַפֵּ֨ר לָהֶ֥ם הָמָ֛ן אֶת־כְּב֥וֹד עָשְׁר֖וֹ וְרֹ֣ב בָּנָ֑יו וְאֵת֩ כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֨ר גִּדְּל֤וֹ הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְאֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְּׂא֔וֹ עַל־הַשָּׂרִ֖ים וְעַבְדֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ὑπέδειξεν αὐτοῖς τὸν πλοῦτον αὐτοῦ καὶ τὴν δόξαν ἣν ὁ βασιλεὺς αὐτῷ περιέθηκεν, καὶ ὡς ἐποίησεν αὐτὸν πρωτεύειν καὶ ἡγεῖσθαι τῆς βασιλείας.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Haman recounted unto them the glory of his riches, and the multitude of his children, and all the things wherein the king had promoted him, and how he had advanced him above the princes and servants of the king.
					\item[NIV] Haman boasted to them about his vast wealth, his many sons, and all the ways the king had honored him and how he had elevated him above the other nobles and officials.
					\item[NRSV] and Haman recounted to them the splendor of his riches, the number of his sons, all the promotions with which the king had honored him, and how he had advanced him above the officials and the ministers of the king.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:12} % *ESTHER-5-12
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-12}

			Haman said moreover, Yea, Esther the queen did let no man come in with the king unto the banquet that she had prepared but myself; and to morrow am I invited unto her also with the king.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּאמֶר֮ הָמָן֒ אַ֣ף לֹא־הֵבִיאָה֩ אֶסְתֵּ֨ר הַמַּלְכָּ֧ה עִם־הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ אֶל־הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֥ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖תָה כִּ֣י אִם־אוֹתִ֑י וְגַם־לְמָחָ֛ר אֲנִ֥י קָֽרוּא־לָ֖הּ עִם־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Ἁμάν Οὐ κέκληκεν ἡ βασίλισσα μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως οὐδένα εἰς τὴν δοχὴν ἀλλ᾽ ἢ ἐμέ· καὶ εἰς τὴν αὔριον κέκλημαι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Haman said moreover, Yea, Esther the queen did let no man come in with the king unto the banquet that she had prepared but myself; and to-morrow also am I invited by her together with the king.
					\item[NIV] “And that’s not all,” Haman added. “I’m the only person Queen Esther invited to accompany the king to the banquet she gave. And she has invited me along with the king tomorrow.
					\item[NRSV] Haman added, “Even Queen Esther let no one but myself come with the king to the banquet that she prepared. Tomorrow also I am invited by her, together with the king.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:13} % *ESTHER-5-13
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-13}

			Yet all this availeth me nothing, so long as I see Mordecai the Jew sitting at the king’s gate.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְכָל־זֶ֕ה אֵינֶ֥נּוּ שֹׁוֶ֖ה לִ֑י בְּכָל־עֵ֗ת אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲנִ֤י רֹאֶה֙ אֶת־מָרְדֳּכַ֣י הַיְּהוּדִ֔י יוֹשֵׁ֖ב בְּשַׁ֥עַר הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ταῦτά μοι οὐκ ἀρέσκει, ὅταν ἴδω Μαρδοχαῖον τὸν Ἰουδαῖον ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yet all this availeth me nothing, so long as I see Mordecai the Jew sitting at the king's gate.
					\item[NIV] But all this gives me no satisfaction as long as I see that Jew Mordecai sitting at the king’s gate.”
					\item[NRSV] Yet all this does me no good so long as I see the Jew Mordecai sitting at the king’s gate.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 5:14} % *ESTHER-5-14
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-5-14}

			Then said Zeresh his wife and all his friends unto him, Let a gallows be made of fifty cubits high, and to morrow speak thou unto the king that Mordecai may be hanged thereon: then go thou in merrily with the king unto the banquet. And the thing pleased Haman; and he caused the gallows to be made.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֹּ֣אמֶר לוֹ֩ זֶ֨רֶשׁ אִשְׁתּ֜וֹ וְכָל־אֹֽהֲבָ֗יו יַֽעֲשׂוּ־עֵץ֮ גָּבֹ֣הַּ חֲמִשִּׁ֣ים אַמָּה֒ וּבַבֹּ֣קֶר ׀ אֱמֹ֣ר לַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וְיִתְל֤וּ אֶֽת־מָרְדֳּכַי֙ עָלָ֔יו וּבֹֽא־עִם־הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ אֶל הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֖ה שָׂמֵ֑חַ וַיִּיטַ֧ב הַדָּבָ֛ר לִפְנֵ֥י הָמָ֖ן וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָעֵֽץ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς αὐτὸν Ζωσάρα ἡ γυνὴ αὐτοῦ καὶ οἱ φίλοι Κοπήτω σοι ξύλον πηχῶν πεντήκοντα, ὄρθρου δὲ εἰπὸν τῷ βασιλεῖ καὶ κρεμασθήτω Μαρδοχαῖος ἐπὶ τοῦ ξύλου· σὺ δὲ εἴσελθε εἰς τὴν δοχὴν σὺν τῷ βασιλεῖ καὶ εὐφραίνου. καὶ ἤρεσεν τὸ ῥῆμα τῷ Ἁμάν, καὶ ἡτοιμάσθη τὸ ξύλον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said Zeresh his wife and all his friends unto him, Let a gallows be made fifty cubits high, and in the morning speak thou unto the king that Mordecai may be hanged thereon: then go thou in merrily with the king unto the banquet. And the thing pleased Haman; and he caused the gallows to be made.
					\item[NIV] His wife Zeresh and all his friends said to him, “Have a pole set up, reaching to a height of fifty cubits, and ask the king in the morning to have Mordecai impaled on it. Then go with the king to the banquet and enjoy yourself.” This suggestion delighted Haman, and he had the pole set up.
					\item[NRSV] Then his wife Zeresh and all his friends said to him, “Let a gallows fifty cubits high be made, and in the morning tell the king to have Mordecai hanged on it; then go with the king to the banquet in good spirits.” This advice pleased Haman, and he had the gallows made.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Esther 6} % *ESTHER-6
	% -----
		\label{ESTHER-6}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:1} % *ESTHER-6-1
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-1}

			On that night could not the king sleep, and he commanded to bring the book of records of the chronicles; and they were read before the king.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּלַּ֣יְלָה הַה֔וּא נָדְדָ֖ה שְׁנַ֣ת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֹּ֗אמֶר לְהָבִ֞יא אֶת־סֵ֤פֶר הַזִּכְרֹנוֹת֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י הַיָּמִ֔ים וַיִּהְי֥וּ נִקְרָאִ֖ים לִפְנֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Ὁ δὲ κύριος ἀπέστησεν τὸν ὕπνον ἀπὸ τοῦ βασιλέως τὴν νύκτα ἐκείνην, καὶ εἶπεν τῷ διδασκάλῳ αὐτοῦ εἰσφέρειν γράμματα μνημόσυνα τῶν ἡμερῶν ἀναγινώσκειν αὐτῷ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] On that night could not the king sleep; and he commanded to bring the book of records of the chronicles, and they were read before the king.
					\item[NIV] That night the king could not sleep; so he ordered the book of the chronicles, the record of his reign, to be brought in and read to him.
					\item[NRSV] On that night the king could not sleep, and he gave orders to bring the book of records, the annals, and they were read to the king.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:2} % *ESTHER-6-2
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-2}

			And it was found written, that Mordecai had told of Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king’s chamberlains, the keepers of the door, who sought to lay hand on the king Ahasuerus.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּמָּצֵ֣א כָת֗וּב אֲשֶׁר֩ הִגִּ֨יד מָרְדֳּכַ֜י עַל־בִּגְתָ֣נָא וָתֶ֗רֶשׁ שְׁנֵי֙ סָרִיסֵ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מִשֹּׁמְרֵ֖י הַסַּ֑ף אֲשֶׁ֤ר בִּקְשׁוּ֙ לִשְׁלֹ֣חַ יָ֔ד בַּמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֲחַשְׁוֵרֽוֹשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εὗρεν δὲ τὰ γράμματα τὰ γραφέντα περὶ Μαρδοχαίου, ὡς ἀπήγγειλεν τῷ βασιλεῖ περὶ τῶν δύο εὐνούχων τοῦ βασιλέως ἐν τῷ φυλάσσειν αὐτοὺς καὶ ζητῆσαι ἐπιβαλεῖν τὰς χεῖρας Ἀρταξέρξῃ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it was found written, that Mordecai had told of Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king's chamberlains, of those that kept the threshold, who had sought to lay hands on the king Ahasuerus.
					\item[NIV] It was found recorded there that Mordecai had exposed Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king’s officers who guarded the doorway, who had conspired to assassinate King Xerxes.
					\item[NRSV] It was found written how Mordecai had told about Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king’s eunuchs, who guarded the threshold, and who had conspired to assassinate King Ahasuerus.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:3} % *ESTHER-6-3
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-3}

			And the king said, What honour and dignity hath been done to Mordecai for this? Then said the king’s servants that ministered unto him, There is nothing done for him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מַֽה־נַּעֲשָׂ֞ה יְקָ֧ר וּגְדוּלָּ֛ה לְמָרְדֳּכַ֖י עַל־זֶ֑ה וַיֹּ֨אמְר֜וּ נַעֲרֵ֤י הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ מְשָׁ֣רְתָ֔יו לֹא־נַעֲשָׂ֥ה עִמּ֖וֹ דָּבָֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ ὁ βασιλεύς Τίνα δόξαν ἢ χάριν ἐποιήσαμεν τῷ Μαρδοχαίῳ; καὶ εἶπαν οἱ διάκονοι τοῦ βασιλέως Οὐκ ἐποίησας αὐτῷ οὐδέν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said, What honor and dignity hath been bestowed on Mordecai for this? Then said the king's servants that ministered unto him, There is nothing done for him.
					\item[NIV] “What honor and recognition has Mordecai received for this?” the king asked. “Nothing has been done for him,” his attendants answered.
					\item[NRSV] Then the king said, “What honor or distinction has been bestowed on Mordecai for this?” The king’s servants who attended him said, “Nothing has been done for him.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:4} % *ESTHER-6-4
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-4}

			And the king said, Who is in the court? Now Haman was come into the outward court of the king’s house, to speak unto the king to hang Mordecai on the gallows that he had prepared for him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ מִ֣י בֶחָצֵ֑ר וְהָמָ֣ן בָּ֗א לַחֲצַ֤ר בֵּית־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַחִ֣יצוֹנָ֔ה לֵאמֹ֣ר לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ לִתְלוֹת֙ אֶֽת־מָרְדֳּכַ֔י עַל־הָעֵ֖ץ אֲשֶׁר־הֵכִ֥ין לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐν δὲ τῷ πυνθάνεσθαι τὸν βασιλέα περὶ τῆς εὐνοίας Μαρδοχαίου, ἰδοὺ Ἁμὰν ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ· εἶπεν δὲ ὁ βασιλεύς Τίς ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ; ὁ δὲ Ἁμὰν εἰσῆλθεν εἰπεῖν τῷ βασιλεῖ κρεμάσαι τὸν Μαρδοχαῖον ἐπὶ τῷ ξύλῳ ᾧ ἡτοίμασεν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said, Who is in the court? Now Haman was come into the outward court of the king's house, to speak unto the king to hang Mordecai on the gallows that he had prepared for him.
					\item[NIV] The king said, “Who is in the court?” Now Haman had just entered the outer court of the palace to speak to the king about impaling Mordecai on the pole he had set up for him.
					\item[NRSV] The king said, “Who is in the court?” Now Haman had just entered the outer court of the king’s palace to speak to the king about having Mordecai hanged on the gallows that he had prepared for him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:5} % *ESTHER-6-5
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-5}

			And the king’s servants said unto him, Behold, Haman standeth in the court. And the king said, Let him come in.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמְר֜וּ נַעֲרֵ֤י הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֵלָ֔יו הִנֵּ֥ה הָמָ֖ן עֹמֵ֣ד בֶּחָצֵ֑ר וַיֹּ֥אמֶר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יָבֽוֹא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπαν οἱ διάκονοι τοῦ βασιλέως Ἰδοὺ Ἁμὰν ἕστηκεν ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ· καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεύς Καλέσατε αὐτόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king's servants said unto him, Behold, Haman standeth in the court. And the king said, Let him come in.
					\item[NIV] His attendants answered, “Haman is standing in the court.” “Bring him in,” the king ordered.
					\item[NRSV] So the king’s servants told him, “Haman is there, standing in the court.” The king said, “Let him come in.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:6} % *ESTHER-6-6
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-6}

			So Haman came in. And the king said unto him, What shall be done unto the man whom the king delighteth to honour? Now Haman thought in his heart, To whom would the king delight to do honour more than to myself?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבוֹא֮ הָמָן֒ וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מַה־לַעֲשׂ֕וֹת בָּאִ֕ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָפֵ֣ץ בִּיקָר֑וֹ וַיֹּ֤אמֶר הָמָן֙ בְּלִבּ֔וֹ לְמִ֞י יַחְפֹּ֥ץ הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת יְקָ֖ר יוֹתֵ֥ר מִמֶּֽנִּי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ ὁ βασιλεὺς τῷ Ἁμάν Τί ποιήσω τῷ ἀνθρώπῳ ὃν ἐγὼ θέλω δοξάσαι; εἶπεν δὲ ἐν ἑαυτῷ ὁ Ἁμάν Τίνα θέλει ὁ βασιλεὺς δοξάσαι εἰ μὴ ἐμέ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Haman came in. And the king said unto him, What shall be done unto the man whom the king delighteth to honor? Now Haman said in his heart, To whom would the king delight to do honor more than to myself?
					\item[NIV] When Haman entered, the king asked him, “What should be done for the man the king delights to honor?” Now Haman thought to himself, “Who is there that the king would rather honor than me?”
					\item[NRSV] So Haman came in, and the king said to him, “What shall be done for the man whom the king wishes to honor?” Haman said to himself, “Whom would the king wish to honor more than me?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:7} % *ESTHER-6-7
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-7}

			And Haman answered the king, For the man whom the king delighteth to honour,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר הָמָ֖ן אֶל־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ אִ֕ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָפֵ֥ץ בִּיקָרֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα Ἄνθρωπον ὃν ὁ βασιλεὺς θέλει δοξάσαι,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Haman said unto the king, For the man whom the king delighteth to honor,
					\item[NIV] So he answered the king, “For the man the king delights to honor,
					\item[NRSV] So Haman said to the king, “For the man whom the king wishes to honor,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:8} % *ESTHER-6-8
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-8}

			Let the royal apparel be brought which the king useth to wear, and the horse that the king rideth upon, and the crown royal which is set upon his head:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָבִ֙יאוּ֙ לְב֣וּשׁ מַלְכ֔וּת אֲשֶׁ֥ר לָֽבַשׁ־בּ֖וֹ הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְס֗וּס אֲשֶׁ֨ר רָכַ֤ב עָלָיו֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר נִתַּ֛ן כֶּ֥תֶר מַלְכ֖וּת בְּרֹאשֽׁוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐνεγκάτωσαν οἱ παῖδες τοῦ βασιλέως στολὴν βυσσίνην ἣν ὁ βασιλεὺς περιβάλλεται, καὶ ἵππον ἐφ᾽ ὃν ὁ βασιλεὺς ἐπιβαίνει,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] let royal apparel be brought which the king useth to wear, and the horse that the king rideth upon, and on the head of which a crown royal is set:
					\item[NIV] have them bring a royal robe the king has worn and a horse the king has ridden, one with a royal crest placed on its head.
					\item[NRSV] let royal robes be brought, which the king has worn, and a horse that the king has ridden, with a royal crown on its head.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:9} % *ESTHER-6-9
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-9}

			And let this apparel and horse be delivered to the hand of one of the king’s most noble princes, that they may array the man withal whom the king delighteth to honour, and bring him on horseback through the street of the city, and proclaim before him, Thus shall it be done to the man whom the king delighteth to honour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְנָת֨וֹן הַלְּב֜וּשׁ וְהַסּ֗וּס עַל־יַד־אִ֞ישׁ מִשָּׂרֵ֤י הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַֽפַּרְתְּמִ֔ים וְהִלְבִּ֙ישׁוּ֙ אֶת־הָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָפֵ֣ץ בִּֽיקָר֑וֹ וְהִרְכִּיבֻ֤הוּ עַל־הַסּוּס֙ בִּרְח֣וֹב הָעִ֔יר וְקָרְא֣וּ לְפָנָ֔יו כָּ֚כָה יֵעָשֶׂ֣ה לָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָפֵ֥ץ בִּיקָרֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ δότω ἑνὶ τῶν φίλων τοῦ βασιλέως τῶν ἐνδόξων καὶ στολισάτω τὸν ἄνθρωπον ὃν ὁ βασιλεὺς ἀγαπᾷ, καὶ ἀναβιβασάτω αὐτὸν ἐπὶ τὸν ἵππον, καὶ κηρυσσέτω διὰ τῆς πλατείας τῆς πόλεως λέγων Οὕτως ἔσται παντὶ ἀνθρώπῳ ὃν ὁ βασιλεὺς δοξάζει.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and let the apparel and the horse be delivered to the hand of one of the king's most noble princes, that they may array the man therewith whom the king delighteth to honor, and cause him to ride on horseback through the street of the city, and proclaim before him, Thus shall it be done to the man whom the king delighteth to honor.
					\item[NIV] Then let the robe and horse be entrusted to one of the king’s most noble princes. Let them robe the man the king delights to honor, and lead him on the horse through the city streets, proclaiming before him, ‘This is what is done for the man the king delights to honor!’”
					\item[NRSV] Let the robes and the horse be handed over to one of the king’s most noble officials; let him robe the man whom the king wishes to honor, and let him conduct the man on horseback through the open square of the city, proclaiming before him: ‘Thus shall it be done for the man whom the king wishes to honor.’”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:10} % *ESTHER-6-10
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-10}

			Then the king said to Haman, Make haste, and take the apparel and the horse, as thou hast said, and do even so to Mordecai the Jew, that sitteth at the king’s gate: let nothing fail of all that thou hast spoken.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ לְהָמָ֗ן מַ֠הֵר קַ֣ח אֶת־הַלְּב֤וּשׁ וְאֶת־הַסּוּס֙ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבַּ֔רְתָּ וַֽעֲשֵׂה־כֵן֙ לְמָרְדֳּכַ֣י הַיְּהוּדִ֔י הַיּוֹשֵׁ֖ב בְּשַׁ֣עַר הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ אַל־תַּפֵּ֣ל דָּבָ֔ר מִכֹּ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּֽרְתָּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἶπεν δὲ ὁ βασιλεὺς τῷ Ἁμάν Καλῶς ἐλάλησας· οὕτως ποίησον τῷ Μαρδοχαίῳ τῷ Ἰουδαίῳ τῷ θεραπεύοντι ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ, καὶ μὴ παραπεσάτω σου λόγος ὧν ἐλάλησας.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then the king said to Haman, Make haste, and take the apparel and the horse, as thou hast said, and do even so to Mordecai the Jew, that sitteth at the king's gate: let nothing fail of all that thou hast spoken.
					\item[NIV] “Go at once,” the king commanded Haman. “Get the robe and the horse and do just as you have suggested for Mordecai the Jew, who sits at the king’s gate. Do not neglect anything you have recommended.”
					\item[NRSV] Then the king said to Haman, “Quickly, take the robes and the horse, as you have said, and do so to the Jew Mordecai who sits at the king’s gate. Leave out nothing that you have mentioned.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:11} % *ESTHER-6-11
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-11}

			Then took Haman the apparel and the horse, and arrayed Mordecai, and brought him on horseback through the street of the city, and proclaimed before him, Thus shall it be done unto the man whom the king delighteth to honour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקַּ֤ח הָמָן֙ אֶת־הַלְּב֣וּשׁ וְאֶת־הַסּ֔וּס וַיַּלְבֵּ֖שׁ אֶֽת־מָרְדֳּכָ֑י וַיַּרְכִּיבֵ֙הוּ֙ בִּרְח֣וֹב הָעִ֔יר וַיִּקְרָ֣א לְפָנָ֔יו כָּ֚כָה יֵעָשֶׂ֣ה לָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָפֵ֥ץ בִּיקָרֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἔλαβεν δὲ Ἁμὰν τὴν στολὴν καὶ τὸν ἵππον, καὶ ἐστόλισεν τὸν Μαρδοχαῖον καὶ ἀνεβίβασεν αὐτὸν ἐπὶ τὸν ἵππον, καὶ διῆλθεν διὰ τῆς πλατείας τῆς πόλεως καὶ ἐκήρυσσεν λέγων Οὕτως ἔσται παντὶ ἀνθρώπῳ ὃν ὁ βασιλεὺς θέλει δοξάσαι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then took Haman the apparel and the horse, and arrayed Mordecai, and caused him to ride through the street of the city, and proclaimed before him, Thus shall it be done unto the man whom the king delighteth to honor.
					\item[NIV] So Haman got the robe and the horse. He robed Mordecai, and led him on horseback through the city streets, proclaiming before him, “This is what is done for the man the king delights to honor!”
					\item[NRSV] So Haman took the robes and the horse and robed Mordecai and led him riding through the open square of the city, proclaiming, “Thus shall it be done for the man whom the king wishes to honor.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:12} % *ESTHER-6-12
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-12}

			And Mordecai came again to the king’s gate. But Haman hasted to his house mourning, and having his head covered.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֥שָׁב מָרְדֳּכַ֖י אֶל־שַׁ֣עַר הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְהָמָן֙ נִדְחַ֣ף אֶל־בֵּית֔וֹ אָבֵ֖ל וַחֲפ֥וּי רֹֽאשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐπέστρεψεν δὲ ὁ Μαρδοχαῖος εἰς τὴν αὐλήν· Ἁμὰν δὲ ὑπέστρεψεν εἰς τὰ ἴδια λυπούμενος κατὰ κεφαλῆς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Mordecai came again to the king's gate. But Haman hasted to his house, mourning and having his head covered.
					\item[NIV] Afterward Mordecai returned to the king’s gate. But Haman rushed home, with his head covered in grief,
					\item[NRSV] Then Mordecai returned to the king’s gate, but Haman hurried to his house, mourning and with his head covered.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:13} % *ESTHER-6-13
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-13}

			And Haman told Zeresh his wife and all his friends every thing that had befallen him. Then said his wise men and Zeresh his wife unto him, If Mordecai be of the seed of the Jews, before whom thou hast begun to fall, thou shalt not prevail against him, but shalt surely fall before him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְסַפֵּ֨ר הָמָ֜ן לְזֶ֤רֶשׁ אִשְׁתּוֹ֙ וּלְכָל־אֹ֣הֲבָ֔יו אֵ֖ת כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר קָרָ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֩אמְרוּ֩ ל֨וֹ חֲכָמָ֜יו וְזֶ֣רֶשׁ אִשְׁתּ֗וֹ אִ֣ם מִזֶּ֣רַע הַיְּהוּדִ֡ים מָרְדֳּכַ֞י אֲשֶׁר֩ הַחִלּ֨וֹתָ לִנְפֹּ֤ל לְפָנָיו֙ לֹא־תוּכַ֣ל ל֔וֹ כִּֽי־נָפ֥וֹל תִּפּ֖וֹל לְפָנָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ διηγήσατο Ἁμὰν τὰ συμβεβηκότα αὐτῷ Ζωσάρᾳ τῇ γυναικὶ αὐτοῦ καὶ τοῖς φίλοις· καὶ εἶπαν πρὸς αὐτὸν οἱ φίλοι καὶ ἡ γυνή Εἰ ἐκ γένους Ἰουδαίων Μαρδοχαῖος, ἦρξαι ταπεινοῦσθαι ἐνώπιον αὐτοῦ, πεσὼν πεσῇ· οὐ μὴ δύνῃ αὐτὸν ἀμύνασθαι, ὅτι θεὸς ζῶν μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Haman recounted unto Zeresh his wife and all his friends everything that had befallen him. Then said his wise men and Zeresh his wife unto him, If Mordecai, before whom thou hast begun to fall, be of the seed of the Jews, thou shalt not prevail against him, but shalt surely fall before him.
					\item[NIV] and told Zeresh his wife and all his friends everything that had happened to him. His advisers and his wife Zeresh said to him, “Since Mordecai, before whom your downfall has started, is of Jewish origin, you cannot stand against him—you will surely come to ruin!”
					\item[NRSV] When Haman told his wife Zeresh and all his friends everything that had happened to him, his advisers and his wife Zeresh said to him, “If Mordecai, before whom your downfall has begun, is of the Jewish people, you will not prevail against him, but will surely fall before him.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Esther 6:14} % *ESTHER-6-14
		% % ---
			\label{ESTHER-6-14}

			And while they were yet talking with him, came the king’s chamberlains, and hasted to bring Haman unto the banquet that Esther had prepared.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עוֹדָם֙ מְדַבְּרִ֣ים עִמּ֔וֹ וְסָרִיסֵ֥י הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ הִגִּ֑יעוּ וַיַּבְהִ֙לוּ֙ לְהָבִ֣יא אֶת־הָמָ֔ן אֶל־הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשְׂתָ֥ה אֶסְתֵּֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἔτι αὐτῶν λαλούντων παραγίνονται οἱ εὐνοῦχοι ἐπισπεύδοντες τὸν Ἁμὰν ἐπὶ τὸν πότον ὃν ἡτοίμασεν Ἐσθήρ.}
